\newcommand{\ArtTile}[3]{%
  \begin{minipage}[t]{0.31\linewidth}
    \centering
    \includegraphics[width=\linewidth,height=0.32\textheight,keepaspectratio]{#1}\\[-0.25ex]
    {\footnotesize\itshape #2}\\[-0.25ex]
    {\scriptsize\verb|\StructurePic{#1}{#2}{...}|}
  \end{minipage}%
}
